# Full Review — 2026-09-05 (NEW BASELINE, Fable 5.1, Day 0 of cycle 16)

**Run:** `wf_2542f74b-b0d` · **Artifacts:** this file + `fullreview_grades_2026-09-05.json` (suffix-free — resets both `fullreview-delta` and `fullreview-full`) · **Model identity:** Fable 5.1 for every grader and verifier

## Method

NEW BASELINE (suffix-free; resets fullreview-delta and fullreview-full). Fable 5.1 graded every one of the 17 panel rows from scratch, one finding-verifier batch per ≤8 findings (finding-verifier agent, lean REFUTED), model identity held for graders and verifiers. Invalidation of the delta chain: #3245 rewrote the review-skill corpus (102 files) on 2026-08-27; the calendar's dated hold required this run to be a full baseline. Ground truth graded: cycle 16 DAY 0 (genesis 2026-09-05; the pre-start countdown was the live surface), budget tier 0, repo PUBLIC, main a16d4368a at launch with the #3483 test-only fix in the working tree; main moved by #3483 (17143485c) during the panel and the artifact records that. Anchors loaded VERBATIM per lens from the newest prior artifact that graded it (2026-08-16 delta / 08-09 delta / 08-02 delta / 07-28 partial); extensions are stated, never redefinitions. Seeded: the handover's six instruments unable to fail, REGRADE_2026-09-01_FABLE's gap list, the CALCULATION_PROOF_2026-09-02 P1s, #3480, the janitor cadence measurement, the phrase-matched prereg test. Interruption, recorded: the first launch hit the 5-hour session limit at ~70 min (16 agents done, 17 dead); resumed from the Workflow cache after the reset — 38/38 agents completed, 0 errors; ~7.2M subagent tokens across both runs (3.76M + 3.40M). Read-only contract held through Phase 3; the one live repair during the run (the #3485 chronicle resurrection) is recorded on that issue and in INCIDENT_LOG, not in this grade.

## Headline

Seventeen rows graded from scratch on Day 0 of cycle 16 and no A- survives: 13 rows fell one notch or more from their carried-forward grades (cto A-→B+, principal A-→B+, aiq B+→B, cpo B+→B-, designer A-→B+, dataviz B+→B, narrative B+→C+, a11y A-→B+, reader B+→B-, observability A-→B+, data-architect B+→B, integrations A-→B+, devex A-→B+), 1 rose (qs C+→B-), 3 held (security, cost, growth). The delta chain had been flattering: every carried grade came from a run that measured a change surface, and a from-scratch look at the whole product on its emptiest day is a harsher instrument by design. The review earned its keep the same night: four rows independently found that the chronicle auto-publish sweep had republished cycle 15's tombstoned Week-1 draft on Day 0 (#3485, repaired live within the run). 99 findings CONFIRMED, 5 PLAUSIBLE, 12 REFUTED — a 10% first-pass refutation rate against the historical ~50%; three grades moved on verifier dissent (qs, security, data-architect), recorded rather than averaged.

## Process verdict — which layer should have caught it

- **cto** — Two rituals should have caught the confirmed findings. (1) The /prove-it discipline ('what would make this fail?') applied to the alarm set: site-api-ai-errors and telegram-worker-errors were adopted with a threshold copied from a different surface (serve_stack.py:176) and never asked what traffic could trip them — the #3413 postmortem's own lesson ('count VERDICTS not latency; a cap sized from a comment never succeeds') was applied to the client cap but not to the alarm guarding the same surface. Gate to add: tests/test_alarm_threshold_reachability.py (threshold ≤ measured median hourly traffic, or rate-based, or dated registry exemption). (2) The wrap's e10 citation gate passed on 09-03 and 09-04 while qa-smoke-failures was lit for causes its citation says were cured — the gate matches alarm NAME to an entry, never cause to cause. Extend check_alarm_citations.py with a `cause` comparison against the alarm's live StateReason/check id, and extend the #3423 seed's own guard so the escalation channel is a routing contract, not a bar inside two readers that run on the wrong clock. The reset pipeline's post-override checklist also lacks a frozen-artifact sweep (the #1985 class recurred on the third reset).
- **principal** — The PR layer would have caught most of these — but 26 of 29 red Unit-Test runs on main in 14 days never went through it: they were direct pushes (the reset pipeline, wrap/docs commits, and fix commits), and a direct push meets only the pre-commit format hook. The layer to add is a pre-push test gate on the direct-push path (premerge lane + derived artifact-reader set) and a pytest leg in the reset sweep; the ritual to extend is /wrap and /incident: #3477's closure shipped the JS leg only and its unchecked box (regenerate the 2941 fixtures) named the very class that recurred on 09-04, so 'closed' should have required the python leg and a derived fixture. Separately, the review-discipline rule 'a guard needs a positive control' is a memory entry, not a gate — 35 of 101 new source-scanning guards skipped it.
- **aiq** — The reader-truth judge caught AIQ-3 and AIQ-5 today — but only in the advisory standalone lane that auto-files (#3480), hours after readers had the content, and the deterministic layer had no rule for either (grounding classes cover numbers/dates/freshness/behavioral/night, none cover tense or undated config evidence). AIQ-1 shows the #3050 guard pinned one of the three relations its own docstring states; AIQ-2 shows #2973 hardened the exception path but not the parse path of the same judges. The gate to add: (1) the pre-start temporal rule as a registered freshness-class member in tests/grounding_wiring.py so it is in the census, not a convention; (2) UNEVALUATED semantics for no-verdict/truncated replies in both judges, proven by a must-fail fixture (/prove-it: 'what would make this red' on parse_verdict); (3) extend the #3050 AST guard to the full ordering; (4) the /new-machinery check on #3414 should have named the missing dead-man at landing — add the BoardQualityGateVerdict channel to the heartbeat census.
- **cpo** — CPO-1 belonged to two existing rituals and escaped both: the #2109 phase-filter ratchet (extended by #3444 two days ago) does not see chronicle_approve_lambda's raw table.query, and restart_verify (23 checks) asserts the lead-ins but never that the auto-publish window is empty of drafts post-reset — so add the ratchet member and the verify assertion, and make the publish path re-derive rather than replay a stored snapshot; the Story door then also needs its charter dead-man (a premiere-sweep check in tests/visual_qa.py that would have gone red at 18:01Z). CPO-2 belonged to observability and to the prove-it discipline: a grader that logged 'kept=0 broken=0' for 21 days had no metric and no alarm, and its only test mocked the function under test — the gate to add is the 'vacuous positive control' rule from the review-discipline index applied to every deterministic grader (a test must drive the real evaluator to a non-inconclusive verdict) plus a dead-man on every grader's output count.
- **designer** — The post-deploy visual-qa mobile pass (tests/visual_qa.py, gating) and the pre-merge tests/pr_render_gate.py 390x844 pass both render every page at phone width and assert overflow, reveals, app-bar fit and tap targets — but neither asserts the initial scroll position, so DES-1 has been live since #151 unmeasured; and the HTML text-floor audit was deliberately armed on 3 pages (#2674, 'measure clean first') with no clock to extend it, so DES-2 was left as a known-but-unscheduled gap that the DESIGN_SYSTEM_V5 §10.5 prose then over-claimed. Gate to add: (a) `scrollY == 0` + hero-in-first-viewport at 390 in both harnesses over the qa_manifest registry; (b) TEXT_FLOOR_PAGES derived from qa_manifest (tier 1–2) after the 19 tokens.css rules are lifted; (c) check_css_tokens.py sweeps tokens.css component rules and site/assets/js/*.js (hex + matchMedia literals) — the #1974 'the drift class moved to the surface the gate never reached' lesson, applied one surface further out.
- **dataviz** — The two QA layers that touch charts — the Playwright visual sweep (render/overflow) and the reader-truth judge (prose) — cannot see encoding arithmetic: a goal in a y-domain, two series at different x for one date, a hard-coded n beside a computed one, a probability whose numerator is all-censored. Nothing exercises charts.js against fixtures at all (only tests/test_constellation_edge_cpts_1215.py imports it). Gate to add: a Node-executed charts.js contract test in the Unit Tests job (DV path-to-A #1) as the 'contract' primitive for the shared builder, plus an API-prose rule extending the #2003 doc-facts idea — any 'n=<int>' literal in a served string must equal a numeric field in the same payload (DV-1's stale 'n=2' would have redded on the day it went stale). The pre-start contract sweep should own absence-as-zero on genesis-clamped windows (DV-4/5/9) so a reset cannot re-mint them.
- **qs** — The reader-truth/visual-QA layer caught the symptom (#3480, the MADE date) but no layer compares the served ledger with the sealed artifact, and no gate models the pre-start window as a phase: the missing primitives are (1) a CONTRACT test between the freeze (registry) and /api/predictions (consumer) — season rows ⊆ frozen ids, provenance fields present on the wire — run by restart_verify and CI, and (2) a DERIVATION GUARD that write-time phase derives from date vs genesis rather than a constant (the same class as #1952's wall-clock week_id). The pooled-skill label (QS-3) should have been caught by test_calibration_core_parity with a two-strata negative control; the point-claim class (QS-4) by an emission-contract prove-red fixture alongside #3046's. Register both defect classes in docs/CONVENTIONS.md §9 with their owning gate.
- **narrative** — Layer 2 (qa_smoke_lambda, nightly) should own a deterministic contract between /journal/posts.json and the set of visible chronicle installments — it has a podcast-parity check (10d) and a permalink-blackhole check (10f) on this exact feed but nothing that asks 'is every listed post allowed to exist in this cycle'. restart_verify (the reset's own gate) proved the manifest at reset time and stopped; the #3477 sweep discipline ('run the whole gate, not a subset') needs a time dimension: re-verify the narrative feed after the first scheduled writer runs (18:00Z), and the #3390 Day-1 runlist should carry that re-curl. In CHARTER terms the missing primitive is the derivation guard — the sweep re-derived 'publishable' from a status field instead of from the phase registry every other reader consults — and the ratchet: the #946/#1085/#1200 family fixed three readers one at a time; a set-guard (every DDB reader/writer of an EXPERIMENT_SCOPED partition must route through with_phase_filter/singleton_visible, enforced by AST over lambdas/) would have caught this writer before a reset found it.
- **security** — SEC-1 should have been caught by the NEW_SIGNAL_PLAYBOOK/ADR-154 'privacy tier BEFORE the first write' step applied to reader-input doors — the two doors were added without a DATA_GOVERNANCE row or a prefix decision, and deploy/pii_surface_guard.py names generated/ as the gap in its own docstring while scanning only site/. Gate to add: a derivation guard over the reader-input write sites (every put_capture_record Key vs the bucket policy's anonymous-read prefixes), enrolled in the pre-merge conftest list next to test_privacy_tier_wiring_2803.py. SEC-2/SEC-3 are guard-the-instance specimens: extend test_function_url_origin_header_validation.py to the healthz special-case and the #1905 real-name scanner to config/, so each guard covers its SET.
- **a11y** — The layer that should have caught a11y-1/2/6 is the #1433 axe gate — but it compares against a baseline that accepted them on 08-02 and gates only on NEW (page, rule-id) keys, so a serious rule with hundreds of nodes is permanent debt with no owner; the layer that should have caught a11y-3 is the standalone visual-qa fire, which DID print the shrink line every day (7, 7, 5, 11 candidates on the runs sampled) into an advisory job that is usually red for reader-truth reasons and that nothing downstream reads. In charter terms the ledger has a registry and a contract but no ratchet (entries only shrink when a human remembers) and no dead-man (a stale-in-good-direction entry pages nobody). Gates to add: (1) tests/test_a11y_audit.py reds on any serious/critical ledger entry without an `issue` field (the #2956 truth-ledger rule, reused); (2) the /wrap gate battery reads the latest visual-qa artifact's shrink list and reds on candidates older than 7 days; (3) a phase_dependent flag on EXPERIMENT_SCOPED pages so a reset can never masquerade as a fix; (4) a cadence test pinning a light-context axe pass at deploy time.
- **reader** — The reset's gate battery (rendered 96/96, semantic 8/8, truth clean at ~16:5xZ) was a point-in-time check; nothing re-asserts the served chronicle after later scheduled writers run, and the wipe left the cycle-15 draft with status=draft so the sweep's own age-only guard could not tell a 'forgot to click' draft from a tombstoned one. Layer that should own it: restart_intelligence_wipe (void drafts it tombstones) + the nightly qa_smoke_lambda (dead-man: served /journal/posts.json == DDB visible chronicle set) + the sweep's structural phase/tombstone filter. For the cast fork, the pre-merge cast guard's scan set (tests/test_cast_roster_consistency.py PROMPT_LITERAL_DIRS) must include deploy/ and config/, and the prereg freeze must refuse a non-operational coach_name before the seal. For the odds figure, ADR-105's 'uncertainty + n on every statistical claim' needs a unit-level bound test on the survival rollup, not a code comment.
- **observability** — All three P2/P3 headline findings should have been caught by an existing ritual: (1) /prove-it on #1956/#2378 closure — the check shipped fail-soft and was closed on the grant being MERGED, not on a numeric rate ever appearing in a qa-smoke log; add to the closure comment template a required 'first non-degraded output observed at <timestamp>' line for any fail-soft instrument. (2) The /wrap alarm-citation gate — its rules are existence + #N-openness + recurrence-negative wording; add the structural timestamp rule (citation.added vs StateTransitionedTimestamp) so a re-fired alarm cannot be green under an old cure. (3) The remediation agent's own report — it already prints the #1960 renewal ratchet; the gate to add is 'a needs-human line that appears verbatim in ≥3 consecutive reports must resolve to an issue number or be dropped' (the class-level fix for the stale-secrets, cadence-gap and IAM-drift lines). Additionally the #3390 guard shipped as a single-file pin — the /new-machinery 'guard the set' question should have been asked at the PR.
- **cost** — Two rituals should have caught these. (1) The monthly close (scripts/monthly_close.py, #3375) reads tier residence and the CallerClass split but never counts surge flips or compares SURGE_UNIQUES_THRESHOLD to the month's uniques distribution — five owner emails and an SSM history were the only record; add a 'surge flips this month + threshold vs baseline p95' line to the close and a dead-man alarm on flip count. (2) When #2892 changed what ProjectedMonthlySpend means, the change was made 'auditable' only on the ops dashboard; the receipts contract test (tests/test_receipts_endpoint.py) should have been extended in the same PR to assert the public payload states the projection's scope — a public-number-scope check belongs in that test file and in tests/api_schemas/api_receipts.json. The same test file also pins a false sentence (the per_feature_note), which is the phrase-pinned-prose class the principal row is grading: the gate to extend is 'a test that asserts a disclaimer must assert the CONDITION the disclaimer claims (no EstimatedCostUSD{LambdaFunction} series exists), not the wording'.
- **data-architect** — Three layers should have caught these before a review did. (1) The reset's own verify (restart_verify / the #3390 Day-1 runlist) has no post-reset DATA census: it renders pages and checks alarms but never asserts 'no EXPERIMENT_SCOPED row dated < genesis without phase=pilot', 'no CROSS_PHASE row tombstoned', 'every classify()==EXPERIMENT_SCOPED pk family is in the wipe's covered set', or 'cycle stamps agree with closing_cycle_for_genesis' — add these as a step-N census gate (the same scan step-0 already runs) and make the 48h auto-publish sweep's negative control part of it. (2) The /new-machinery contract primitive was applied producer→consumer (8 pairs) but never writer↔writer, so #3443's fix guarded one instance while the class stayed open (habit_scores' duplicated writer, chronicle's status-vs-tombstone) — extend the registry with a CoOwned entry type and require it whenever a second module gains a put/update on an existing key. (3) Decimal hygiene has no derivation guard at all (no AST gate, no dead-man on 'attempting' ledger rows), so a float crash after an email send was invisible for 5 days until a review's Logs Insights query — add the AST gate to CI's lint job and the ledger dead-man to nightly qa-smoke. The through-line matches Session U's: instruments (assert_registry_coverage, the stamp audit, the recap day-guard, the sweep's 10-day 'pre-genesis leftover' guard) that enumerate from a hand-typed list or floor their comparand to the passing value, and therefore cannot fail on the case they were written for.
- **integrations** — INT-1 should have been caught by the /prove-it reflex at #1480's merge ('fail-closed paths need a LIVE proof'): the PR's tests used a FakeDdbTable so a denied put_item was unreachable, and the deployed function has logged the denial at ERROR on every run since — but the only alarm on the checker watches AWS/Lambda Errors, and no post-deploy step reads a function's own ERROR lines. Gate to add: (1) extend the SEC-01 write-scope contract (tests/test_site_api_write_scope.py) from the site-api to every LeadingKeys-scoped role in role_policies*.py, so a new sentinel on a partition outside the allowlist fails CI before it ships; (2) a logged-ERROR MetricFilter dead-man on the operational checkers (the monitoring_stack.py:993 pattern), routed to the digest. INT-3 should have been caught by the 'guard the SET, not the instance' review rule at #2877: the class fix was applied to one member of a hand-typed set the enumeration-drift linter does not cover — the gate is to bring DAILY_SOURCES under test_source_enumeration_drift.py and derive it. The seed (INT-2) is the same class in classifier form: any consumer that names the registry as its authority must be tested against a registry-derived parametrization, not a fixture.
- **devex** — The /wrap battery (scripts/wrap_gates.py) and the /land skill are the layers that should have caught DEVEX-1..4: both run on every session and both consume the instruments that are wrong. Gate to add: a 'mirror-derivation contract' test family (tests/test_docs_ci_owns_doc_gates.py or a sibling) asserting, for every local check that names a CI job as its target — wrap_gates, restart_verify_gates, wait_pr_green, the pre-commit hook — that its step/check set is a superset of the set parsed from the workflow YAML; plus a /prove-it item in the wrap skill: 'what would make main-green read RED on a run whose rollup is cancelled?' with the two fixture runs from this review as the must-fail cases. The /incident skill should record one INCIDENT_LOG row for the class 'a cancelled rollup over a real red' naming BOTH config mechanisms (deploy-group pending-slot cancel; docs-ci cancel-in-progress on main), since the memory note reference_a_cancelled_ci_rollup_hides_real_failures.md records the symptom ('open the steps') without the cause, and a lesson without a gate is the C-anchor's 'tribal knowledge from memory topics'.
- **growth** — Three layers should have caught these and did not: (1) the reset's own verifier (restart_verify + the #3477 sweep) checks 96 registered URLs but never asks 'what ELSE 200s under /journal/posts/' — add the assertion that every live week-NN permalink is in posts.json (a negative control with a planted stale page); (2) the nightly qa-smoke / reader-truth judge has no content-provenance check — add 'newest posts.json/moments entry must not predate genesis while day_n ≥ 0' (#3485's own acceptance item) and 'moments/index.json ⊆ posts.json'; (3) the #2254 PR shipped a new DynamoDB verb with no IAM-parity test although the pattern exists (test_freshness_checker_iam_parity) — make the /land skill's checklist require an IAM-parity assertion whenever a Lambda gains a new AWS verb, and require a WARNING-level log (never INFO) on any swallowed write. The promise-copy class (G-4, G-6) needs the doc-facts gate extended to reader-facing templates and page copy, with the sender registry as the owner.

## Per-lens detail

### cto — CTO / architect — **B+** (prior A-)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-08-16_delta.json):** A — Every resilience/ops claim in ARCHITECTURE.md reproduces against live CDK + AWS state; liveness sensors are capability-verified (not just IAM-parity-verified) after deploy; no alarm sits in ALARM unexplained beyond ~72h; the ADR-103 ledger rows match live subsystem state — all inside the solo-operator $85/mo envelope with no added standing machinery.

_Extension:_ EXTENDED (not redefined), per seed cto-S1: (1) the envelope in the A anchor is now the ADR-133 #2801 base of $215/mo (surge $252) effective 2026-09-01 — live Budgets limit reads 215.0, forecast $130.79, tier 0 — so 'no added standing machinery' is judged against that ceiling; (2) the A anchor additionally requires that a READER-AUDIENCE red reaches a human within one detection interval of the probe that raised it, independent of any session or wrap running — the 09-01 outage showed 'immediate detection, ~31h escalation' is not A; (3) 'unexplained' in the 72h clause now includes 'explained by a citation whose stated cause is not the alarm's current cause' — a rotted citation is worse than none for the gate that reads it.

**Trend:** A- (2026-08-16 delta) → B+: the prior grade predates the launch-day reader outage; this baseline reproduced that its fix (#3423) moved the escalation bar into two readers that run on a slower or inverted clock while the alarm's live routing is unchanged, found two Errors alarms structurally unable to fire on the surfaces they guard, three unreproducible resilience claims in ARCHITECTURE.md, and a lit alarm explained by a citation for a different, cured cause — against a data-protection, IAM, stack and cost posture that reproduces cleanly at the extended $215 envelope.

**Confirmed findings**

- **CTO-1** P2 [A/S] — Reader-facing outage escalation is still bound to the READING cadence, not the detection: the only probes of the public AI path (ai-canary-overall / ai-canary-blind) route to the once-daily 15:00Z digest, the #3423 'first-red' bar lives only in the wrap gate (session-bound) and in the remediation agent whose cron (14:45Z Mon/Wed/Fri) runs 95 minutes BEFORE the canary that would light the alarm (16:20Z Mon/Wed/Fri). In September's no-session posture a Friday-afternoon /api/board_ask failure is detected Monday (~72h), digested Tuesday (~94h) and agent-escalated Wednesday (~118h) — worse than the 34h the incident row records.

### principal — Principal engineer — **B+** (prior A-)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-08-16_delta.json):** A — Full offline suite green and fast with creds blanked; every stated quality gate (coverage floor, mypy tier set, deploy-critical lane, format gates) matches measured reality within a small tolerance; no unreferenced code shipping in the every-bundle shared modules; exactly one canonical implementation per platform invariant (float→Decimal, Pacific-date, ISO parse); golden tests pinned against the known wall-clock defect class.

_Extension:_ EXTENSION (not a redefinition): A additionally requires that EVERY landing path onto main — PR merge or direct push — meets at least the pre-merge lane before the commit is on main, and that a guard which scans source (AST/regex) ships with a synthetic must-fail control. C additionally covers the case where the gates are sound in construction but the landing path most commits actually use bypasses them, so reds are measured on main rather than prevented (this run: 26 of 29 red Unit-Test runs in 14 days were direct pushes).

**Trend:** A- (2026-08-16) → B+: the gate estate got stronger since (measured-coverage high-water ratchet, full-suite pre-merge job, gate census with mutation verdicts, whole-surface mypy), but measured landing reality moved the other way — 29 red Unit-Test runs on main in 14 days, 26 of them via direct pushes no CI check can stop, and the same regenerated-artifact test class redding at both resets — which is the C-anchor's 'gates partly decorative' in process form rather than in construction.

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-08-16_delta.json):** A — Every reader-facing AI narrative passes a deterministic grounding gate with the audience-correct fail mode (fail-closed public, keep-best internal), every prompt carries the phase/day truth, a reset leaves zero wiped-cycle narrative reachable on any live endpoint, and live spot-checks find no number, date, or verdict a Day-4 reader couldn't trace to real data.

_Extension:_ Extension (not redefinition): the A anchor's 'live spot-checks' clause is read on Day 0 as 'no claim a Day-0 reader couldn't trace' — a tense claim about a genesis that has not happened, or a cause attributed to a data absence the registry already explains (paused / lag-by-design), counts as untraceable exactly like a fabricated number. The C anchor's 'no test or gate would catch it' is read to include a judge whose no-verdict path returns severity ok.

**Trend:** B+ (2026-08-16) → B: the machinery mostly holds on Day 0 (honest nulls on 15 endpoints, three drafts held rather than published, the Friday re-anchor fully tombstoned), but a live spot-check found two reader-facing coach claims a Day-0 reader could not trace (a past-tense genesis and a 'not yet syncing' story about a permanently paused source) that passed every gate, an undated prior-cycle evidence catalog served as current, one door violating its own hazard-ordering contract, and both AI judges carrying a no-verdict path that reads as a pass and fired five times in 14 days.

NOT covered: any POST to /api/ask, /api/explain or /api/board_ask (so live outputs of those doors are inferred from code, not observed); the Telegram worker's full prompt and its lack of a hazard gate (owner-only channel — left as a posture question, not graded); the MCP coach_chat door; inter-coach dialogue, chronicle, podcast, State-of-Matthew and email narrative generators beyond their census entries; the individual bodies of the 11 SURFACES entries; the canary's 179 truncations in 14d (deterministic-first per ADR-105, so not a quality vacuum — cost row); prompt-cache economics; the vision rubric's image lens; and the known-red test_plan_literal_reconciliation (out of scope by brief). Numbers quoted from the shared context (e.g. calibration n=158) were not re-derived except where a live GET returned a different figure (lifetime n=174 on /api/calibration) — noted, not graded, as it is the accuracy row's fact.

### cpo — CPO — **B-** (prior B+)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-07-28_partial.json):** A — Every door's first screen states its loop role; every live engagement hook (predict-the-week, ask-the-board, chronicle/podcast cadence) is demonstrably alive-or-honestly-absent on ANY day of a cycle including Day 4 of a fresh reset; a shared bare URL lands on the page; each of the 4 audiences finds its surface within one click of Home; the early-cycle state reads as a deliberate season premiere (day counter, pre-registration, prequels, cycle-vs-cycle context).

_Extension:_ EXTENSION (not a redefinition): 'demonstrably alive-or-honestly-absent' is read to include every artifact a hook publishes downstream of its own page — the permalink, the manifest, the 'previously on' recap memory, the share-kit, the subscriber onboarding email. A hook whose page carries an honest banner but which pushes dead-cycle content through those channels is graded at the C anchor. Also extended for the seed: 'the loop is taught' requires that the loop's protocols→data leg has at least one instrument that can grade whether coaching moved behavior; an instrument that exists but has never graded is 'dead' in the F-anchor sense, mitigated to C when the site does not claim it works.

**Trend:** B+ (2026-07-28, Day 1 of cycle 11) → B- now: the premiere machinery genuinely improved (cadence lines, season/career split, the attempts ledger, genesis-seeded predict-the-week — three of the five prior path-to-A items verified live), but a same-week re-anchor let the chronicle-approve sweep resurrect cycle 15's Day-1 installment onto the Day-0 Story door and Home (with its manifest, permalink, recap memory and share-kit), and the one instrument that could evidence the seed's first criterion has graded 0 of 480 commitments in its life — the C-anchor's 'dead-cycle content in a hook' plus a dead hook, against an otherwise A-shaped Day 0.

### designer — Product designer — **B+** (prior A-)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-07-28_partial.json):** A — Every live surface draws color/type/spacing from tokens.css; the 11px type floor holds when MEASURED (including SVG text under render scale) at 390 and 1280; single-ember discipline and the breakpoint set are enforced by a gate that sweeps all sheets; Day-N-sparse states are visibly designed and honest.

_Extension:_ EXTENDED (not redefined): the A anchor's 'MEASURED at 390 and 1280' clause now applies to the whole qa_manifest page registry (89 pages), not the 3-page gate scope, and adds: on a 390 viewport every page opens at scrollY 0 with its .page-hero in the first viewport (DESIGN_SYSTEM_V5 §1 'no page is allowed to open cold', measured, not asserted).

**Trend:** A- (2026-07-28, 7 pages rendered) → B+: the prior run measured the floor on the pages the gate covers and found them clean; measuring the full 89-page registry shows the 11px floor is false on 8 live pages (the .provenance trust kit itself is 9.92px) and exposes a mobile-only defect no gate looks for — every evidence-shell page opens 800–1300px down at 390, past its own hero, contradicting §1 and the intro card's 'no page jumps'.

### dataviz — Data-viz expert — **B** (prior B+)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-07-28_partial.json):** A — Every visual encoding is proportional and honest at any n (lengths/areas never anchored to arbitrary floors), every statistical claim carries visible-and-reachable n/CI on touch and keyboard, low-n charts refuse or degrade with explicit copy, and the live site renders zero contradictory encodings on any surface.

_Extension:_ Extension (not redefinition): 'contradictory encodings' includes the same series rendered with two different y-domains on two doors so the caption verdict flips, and a served probability whose numerator is entirely censored observations shorter than its own horizon — a fabricated interval by another name. Also: a literal n in a caption that disagrees with the n the payload computed counts as an F-class 'no n' for that surface, since the reader is given the wrong one.

**Trend:** B+ (2026-07-28, partial) → B: the machinery that earned the B+ (refusals, real-interval-only bands, n on correlations, keyboard readouts) is intact and live, but a from-scratch sweep found one fabricated public probability (47% odds-of-day-30 whose survivors are 100% censored 1–7-day stubs, captioned with a stale 'n=2'), a builder-level date misalignment in dualLineChart, and the results door flattening the weight slope with the very goal-in-domain rule the body hero forbids — three contradictory encodings the A anchor says must be zero.

**Confirmed findings**

**Coverage:** Looked at: all 30 builders in site/assets/js/charts.js (read in full) and every call site across the evidence_* modules, story.js, cockpit.js and evidence_meta.js; motion.js's data-cpts readout (hover/tap/keyboard); tokens.css chart classes; the backend producers for the charts I flagged (site_api_nutrition reconciliation, site_api_rollups survival, site_api_character zeroed state, site_api_physical weekly summary); 25 live /api/* payloads fetched 2026-09-05T00:01–00:18Z; and a headless-Chromium render (Playwright, 1280×900, light theme) of /cockpit/, /data/{character,physical,vitals,habits,sleep,nutrition,training,autonomic,glucose,zone2,labs,ledger,wall}/, /story/, /story/attempts/, /coaching/scorecard/ dumping every figure caption, role=img aria-label and empty-state string (no page JS errors on any of them). Fixture-level Node reproductions for the builder defects that cannot be live on Day 0. Did NOT look at: the OG share-card PNGs (Pillow) or email/brief charts; /data/wall (out of scope by brief); /story/theme-river, /method/mirror and /method/eyeball distributions; calibration-core.js's reliability/scorecard plots beyond the live numbers; mobile viewports and dark-theme contrast; the cockpit's own ring/waveform builders beyond their aria output; MCP tool outputs; any Day-N-with-data state except via fixtures; the full pytest suite (a 40-min run was in progress). No AWS mutation, no Lambda invocation, no working-tree edits.

### qs — QS / biohacker authority — **B-** (prior C+)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-07-28_partial.json):** A — Every public claim is correlative and carries a real, resolvable citation; every protocol lever states the data it targets and the hypothesis that spawned it; every pre-registered bet is either graded or explicitly voided ON THE RECORD across resets; thresholds derive from personal variance or are labelled population constants; every 'how it holds up' surface is either live or honestly retired.

_Extension:_ EXTENSION (not a redefinition): A additionally requires (1) that an aggregate calibration label never claims a property (skilled, well-calibrated) that no constituent stratum has — pooled Brier skill against a pooled base rate is not skill; (2) that the served season ledger is a labelled mirror of the sealed artifact — every row is either in the seal or visibly marked unsealed; (3) that provenance dates on bets are event times (freeze instant, void instant), never lifecycle constants (genesis).

**Trend:** C+ (2026-07-28) → B: the C-anchor failures are gone — 55/55 citations resolve to the claimed papers, cycle 14/15 seals hash-verify live, and every reset's open bets are voided on the record — but the row is held below A- by a pooled career card that reads 'well-calibrated/reliable/skilled' while both strata are unskilled, and by a pre-start window in which Day-0 coach emissions were stamped as cycle-16 season bets that no surface can distinguish from the sealed 14.

### narrative — Narrative editor — **C+** (prior B+)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-08-16_delta.json):** A — Every live narrative surface tells one coherent cycle-6 story — reset seams are invisible to a binge reader, restored prequels are internally reconciled, each coach is distinguishable blind (and the voice-fidelity scoreboard has evidence), and no page contradicts another page's account of the same week.

_Extension:_ Prior extension (2026-08-09, kept verbatim): an A additionally requires that one absence is one story — every surface narrating the same missing data agrees on its duration and cause, states n beside any rate, and never grades a zero-observation set. EXTENDED 2026-09-04 (surface grew — the reset is now a weekly-cadence event with scheduled writers running between reset and Day 1): an A additionally requires that the story the reset tells at reset time is STILL the story every scheduled writer tells at the first cron after it — a surface the reset honestly emptied may not be re-populated by any job that derives 'publishable' from something other than the phase registry.

**Trend:** B+ (2026-08-16) → C+: the static Saturday re-anchor and the prologue reconciliation are genuinely A-grade work, but a daily writer re-published the sealed cycle's Week 1 onto the home page and the story door on the eve of Day 1 — the C anchor's 'stale prior-cycle narratives render on live doors' met literally — and it armed a Day-1 recap the guard cannot see.

### security — Security / privacy lead — **B+** (prior B+)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-08-16_delta.json):** A — Live public JSON exposes zero chronological age / birth year / genome identifiers / vice-substance names (verified by curl+grep, not docs); sensitive S3 prefixes (raw/config/uploads/dashboard) return 403/404 publicly; no live secret or PII in tracked repo files or recent history; the MCP OAuth consent+PKCE gate and DDB-backed per-IP rate limits are provably enforced with no spoofable trust boundary; every Lambda has its own least-privilege role with Resource:* only on actions that cannot be resource-scoped.

_Extension:_ EXTENSION (not a redefinition): the A anchor's 'no PII in tracked repo files' is extended to 'no THIRD-PARTY PII (reader-supplied email/ip_hash) written into an anonymously-readable S3 prefix', because the platform now takes reader input on two public doors and the ADR-046 generated/ prefix is world-readable by bucket policy. A prefix that is public BY DESIGN (generated/*) counts as a 'publicly readable sensitive prefix' the moment a reader-input door writes PII into it.

**Trend:** B+ (2026-08-16) → A-: the two boundaries that held the prior grade down (spoofable client IP #1221, AdministratorAccess cfn-exec #3340) are now proven live to bind (429 on the 11th call under forged XFF; permissions boundary attached), the absolutes are clean across the ENTIRE surface rather than a sample, and the residual is a third-party-PII placement defect in a by-design-public prefix (SEC-1) plus three small guard-set gaps — none of them a bypassable boundary.

### a11y — Accessibility specialist — **B+** (prior A-)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-07-28_partial.json):** A — WCAG AA holds on every live page in BOTH themes (verified by an automated token-contrast check, not hand-written comments); every interactive widget is keyboard-operable with visible focus; data charts are named to AT; reduced-motion is honored; tap targets meet the 44px floor site-wide with a gating audit.

_Extension:_ EXTENSION (not a redefinition): 'verified by an automated token-contrast check' is read to include COMPOSITED text (opacity/color-mix over a surface) and every palette used as text (pillar/coach accents), because the two live AA classes found this run are invisible to a full-opacity token pair check by construction. 'a gating audit' is read to require that the debt ledger the gate compares against is CURRENT: a baselined rule the page no longer exhibits is a disarmed gate, not a guard. 'the 44px floor' is measured against WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8's 24x24 minimum as the hard floor, with 44 as the target — a rule that passes a 2x44 hit box is not a floor.

**Trend:** A- (2026-07-28) → B+: the machinery is as strong as in July (four ledgers now, light and mobile axes added, tap gate non-vacuous), but the July grade was conditional on the ledger shrink landing and staying current — instead the ledger has re-accreted 11 stale keys, the shrink signal still has no consumer (July path-to-A step 4 never landed), the two AA classes on the flagship data pages (opacity-recede text, pillar text in light) were fixed only as instances (#1989, #2919/#3057) while the class survives at ~230 nodes, and no open issue tracks any accessibility debt at all.

**Confirmed findings**

- **a11y-1** P2 [A/M] — The site's 'recede = opacity .55' state grammar drops informational text to 2.2–3.0:1 in both themes on four flagship data pages (~230 axe nodes across the four ledgers), baselined since 08-02 so the gate never fires — two INSTANCES of this class were already fixed (#1989 cockpit .scope-deep, the build-stamp comment) but the class was never swept.

### observability — Observability lead — **B+** (prior A-)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-08-16_delta.json):** A — Every alarm state is honest (red = a real, actionable condition; nothing permanently red or structurally unclearable), every watchdog's producer is verified actually emitting (not just designed to), and the remediation/drift loop demonstrably closes or explicitly hands off every signal it sees — all within the $85 ceiling, urgent-vs-digest split intact, solo operator paged for what matters and only that.

_Extension:_ EXTENSION (not a redefinition): the A clause 'the loop explicitly hands off every signal' is read to require that a hand-off be about the CURRENT state and have a terminal condition — a needs-human line that repeats verbatim every run about a state that a later deploy already replaced, or that the producer can never satisfy by construction, is not a hand-off; it is noise the operator learns to skip. Likewise 'every alarm state is honest' extends to the acknowledgement layers on top of the alarm (docs/alarm_citations.json, remediation-log/ack_ledger.json): a green citation gate over a citation written for a PREVIOUS episode is a dishonest layer on an honest alarm. Ceiling figure in the A anchor is historical ($85); the platform's stated ceiling is now $215/$252 (ADR-133) and the grade uses that.

**Trend:** A- → B+: the alarm estate itself is healthier than at the 08-16 delta (heartbeat pairs complete and live, composites verified routing, all reds true positives, retention bounded), but a from-scratch baseline found a QA watchdog that has never produced its measurement behind a chronic flag (OBS-1), an acknowledgement layer that passes green over a citation written for a previous episode while the agent stores a false 'resolved' (OBS-2), and a self-healing hand-off that repeats an unfillable cadence gap and a superseded drift record every run (OBS-3) — the C-anchor's 'IAM failure swallowed as a warning' and 'reporting green' clauses, each in one specimen.

### cost — Cost engineer — **B+** (prior B+)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-07-28_partial.json):** A — The ADR-063/133 ceiling is enforced at the bedrock_client chokepoint with the ADR-125 audience-ordered ladder, the projection math has no double-count or de-escalation traps, every public and operator-facing cost number (ceiling, monthly total, tier labels) matches the live governor state per ADR-104, and per-feature AI cost is visible on a live public receipt.

_Extension:_ EXTENSION (not a redefinition): 'matches the live governor state' now also requires that a published projection state its SCOPE when the governor extrapolates a subset of spend (#2892 projects prod-cron+remediation only). A correctly derived subset presented to readers as 'projected month-end' is a C-anchor disagreement between public number and governor state, not an A. Second extension: a ceiling that floats on a traffic threshold is only 'enforced' if the threshold sits outside the baseline's own variance (ADR-105 thresholds-from-variance applies to the ceiling's inputs, not just to alarms).

**Trend:** B+ (2026-07-28) → B+: the enforcement core measurably improved (base derived from a measured steady state per #2801, the #2892 class split, per-feature dollar ledger #3374, drift reconciled to ≈1.00) but two honesty gaps opened or went unnoticed since — the public projection is an unlabeled subset that AWS's own forecast contradicts 2x, and the surge threshold has been flipping the ceiling on weekly noise since July — and the per-feature kill switch (#3373) is still absent, so the area holds its grade rather than moving to A-.

**Confirmed findings**

- **COST-01** P2 (defensible; owner may align to the #3474 precedent at P3) [A/S] — SURGE_UNIQUES_THRESHOLD (900) sits at the mean of the current weekly baseline, so 'surge mode' — and with it the effective ceiling and all three tier bands — flips on week-to-week noise: 5 flips in 7 weeks, currently ON at 1011 uniques, loosening the guard 17% ($215→$252; tier-1 trip $157.67→$184.80) on the strength of a 13% weekly bump, not the 'Reddit hit' ADR-133 defined it for.
  - evidence: LifePlatform/Traffic::UniqueVisitors7d weekly readings Aug: 892, 973, 773, 838, 1011 (n=5, mean 897.4, threshold 900). SSM /life-platform/surge-active history: 2026-07-13 true, 07-20 false, 08-10 true, 08-17 false, 08-31 true (each flip = an edge-triggered owner email, cost_governor_lambda.py:807 _alert_surge). ADR-133 derived 900 as '~3.1x the latest reading' of a 165–288 baseline (docs/DECISIONS.md:4317) and its own consequences clause says 'Revisit the threshold as the baseline traffic grows — it is one env var' (DECISIONS.md:4335); the baseline grew 3–4x and nothing carries the revisit: tests only pin the literal (tests/test_cost_governor.py:250-262 parametrize 899/900/901; tests/test_stack_manifest_drift.py:309 asserts manifest == constant). ### data-architect — Data architect — **B** (prior B+)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-08-09_delta.json):** A — Live DynamoDB items, docs/SCHEMA.md, and the phase-taxonomy registry are mutually consistent under spot-sampling; exactly the 2 sanctioned GSIs (ADR-097) exist; the reset machinery is provably total AND idempotent (a double reset preserves each record's original cycle provenance); raw-S3 layouts match their source_registry raw_layout facets including filename scheme.

_Extension:_ Extended, not redefined: (1) 'total' is read against the LIVE pk-family census, not only SOURCE_CLASS — a non-SOURCE family (COACH#<name>) that classify() calls EXPERIMENT_SCOPED but no wipe list visits is a totality escape; (2) 'provenance' includes agreement between a row's cycle stamp and closing_cycle_for_genesis(genesis-in-tombstoned_reason) under an in-place re-anchor; (3) the F 'float write' clause covers a crash that loses a record whose side effect (a sent email, Bedrock spend) already happened; (4) 'tombstoned records leaking' includes WRITER-side resurrection (a status-only selector republishing a tombstoned row), not only reader-side filters.

**Trend:** B+ (2026-08-09) → B: classification totality, GSI discipline, wipe idempotency and the schema doc's headline facts all held under a full-table census, but this run produced live evidence for two C-anchor conditions (a pk family classified wipeable that no wipe or audit visits; cycle stamps that contradict the registry's own derivation after the re-anchor) and one F-anchor instance (an unguarded float write that lost a sent nudge's record), plus a writer-side prior-cycle leak onto the public surface (#3485, filed by other rows) — none of which the 08-09 baseline had in hand.

**Coverage:** Read-only throughout; no Lambda/Bedrock invocation, no AWS mutation, no working-tree edits. DID: two full-table DynamoDB scans (42,597 items, 99 pk families) classified through phase_taxonomy.classify() with buckets for cycle/phase/tombstone anomalies; describe-table GSI census; SSM cycle + budget tier; calibration CALIB#2026-09-0* partition (200 rows); targeted get-item on ~10 rows; live S3 listing of September raw leaves for 15 sources + top-level raw/ and raw/matthew/ prefix census + generated/journal/; CloudWatch filter-log-events on chronicle-approve/email-sender/coach-nudge and a Logs Insights float-rejection query over 50 write-heavy groups (14d, 258,692 records); live GETs on /api/recap, /api/journey, /api/character, /journal/posts.json, /journal/posts/week-0{4,5,6}/, /story/; gh issue reads (open list, last 80 closed, #3485 body, targeted searches); read of phase_taxonomy.py, restart_intelligence_wipe.py, phase_filter.py, chronicle_recap.py, chronicle_approve_lambda.py, site_api_coach_narrative.py, coach_state_updater.py (stamp site), coach_nudge_lambda.py (_finalize), source_registry raw_layout facets, SCHEMA.md (genesis/GSI/family mentions), pair_contract_registry.py names, the two store_habit_scores bodies. DID NOT: run the full pytest suite or any pytest; inspect the 40 MCP modules' write paths for Decimal hygiene; verify each of the ~10 newest-first scoped readers' guards individually (only counted cycle_read_floor adopters); compare SCHEMA.md field tables to live attributes beyond family presence; check raw leaves outside 2026-09 or the whoop/health_auto_export sub-layout months; sample log groups beyond the 50 matched by name (54 Lambdas unsampled for float crashes); read PERSONA#/ENSEMBLE# tombstone contents; independently verify the tagger's '50 rows re-phased' or the 314 count (my census cannot distinguish the 09-03 and 09-04 passes on rows already tombstoned earlier); reconcile my 99-family grouping with the pipeline's '98 pk families' (different grouping — not claimed as a delta); examine GSI2's 6 rows; examine DIL-027 replication or backups. The writer inventory (DA-5) is a static grep/awk heuristic (window ±45 lines around put_item/update_item, then module-level mention) — it over-approximates module mentions and can miss writers that build pks in helpers; the four co-owned records named were each confirmed by reading the write sites.

### integrations — Integrations engineer — **B+** (prior A-)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-08-16_delta.json):** A — Every source's declared cadence/threshold matches its live EventBridge rule with a written derivation (the #471 Todoist standard); the public board and the operator checker tell the same cross-reset-honest story (real last_update + 'dark N days'); a dead OAuth token pages within ~one cadence via alarms that are green because they work, not because they can't fire; dead sources are disclosed everywhere they surface — all with no machinery beyond the one canonical registry.

_Extension:_ EXTENSION (not a redefinition): the A bar's 'green because they work' clause now explicitly covers the checker's OWN sub-instruments (episode dedup, absence markers, sentinel writes) — a write the role cannot perform, logged as 'non-fatal' at ERROR every run, is a dead instrument, and a consumer that claims to derive from the registry must actually read the facet it derives from (behavioral, expected_days), not a hand-rolled proxy.

**Trend:** A- → B+: cadences, thresholds, the public board, token-death paging and webhook dedup all still meet the A anchor, but this baseline found a checker sub-instrument (the #1480 episode dedup) that has been IAM-denied on every run for 49 days with the denial logged 'non-fatal' and invisible to every alarm, plus two guard-the-set gaps (the seed's classifier and the #2643 marker on one of four daily sources) — the platform's recurring 'instruments unable to fail' class, on the ingestion row this time.

**Seed dispositions**

- integrations-S1 — restart_integration_check reds notion stale 607h (behavioral: True, monitored: False) → class: consumer classifies via a hand-rolled proxy (capture_channel in ('mcp','hae')) instead of the registry facet that carries the meaning (behavioral); the #392/#498 enumeration-drift class in classifier form; root cause: deploy/restart_integration_check.py:376-386 routes any non-fresh verdict to FAIL unless `_is_manualish` (:396-398) is true; that helper never reads `behavioral`, and notion's capture_channel is 'notion', not 'mcp'/'hae'. `monitored: False` is also never consulted. Reproduced offline against the live registry: the FAIL branch is reachable for hevy (168h), notion (336h), strava (48h default — no ove; blast radius: 4 behavioral sources; the reset-day operator verdict only (30/32 tonight with one false red); strava's 48h default makes any two rest days before a reset a red. No reader surface, no alarm.; B; guard: Parametrized test over SOURCE_REGISTRY: every behavioral=True source with a stale partition → PASS/'honest no-data'; an infrastructure source stale past threshold → FAIL (positive control). Recommendation: FILE as an S issue (do not waive) — the fix is a one-line facet read and the check's own contr

No Lambda was invoked, no AWS mutation, no working-tree edit.

### devex — DevEx / SDLC lead — **B+** (prior A-)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-07-28_partial.json):** A — Every load-bearing claim an agent reads in its first 5 minutes (CLAUDE.md, CONVENTIONS, the /deploy skill) is either machine-synced or provably current; all deploy paths verifiably stage through one bundle; the fast deploy-critical lane runs green offline in seconds; incident classes are encoded in gates/agents rather than relying on agent memory; drift gates cover process/ownership/date facts, not just counts.

_Extension:_ EXTENSION (not a redefinition): 'incident classes are encoded in gates' is read to include the instruments that READ CI — check_main_green.py / check_ci_warnings.py / wait_pr_green.sh. An A requires that main's own green-reader cannot classify a run carrying a real failing job as 'no signal', and that every local check claiming to mirror a CI job DERIVES its step set from the workflow file (the #3477/#3479 class, generalized) rather than restating a subset.

**Trend:** A- → B+: the deploy plane (one bundle, single-source pins, hook parity) is still A-grade and S2 was fixed at the root, but the run-history read shows main's own green-readers (check_main_green/check_ci_warnings) carry a premise falsified by the 2026-08-02 concurrency redesign, Docs CI cancels ~40% of its own main verdicts by config, and the #3477 'subset of a gate' class was encoded for the reset only — the wrap battery and the merge watch still restate what they claim to mirror, which is the A-anchor's 'incident classes encoded in gates rather than agent memory' failing in the exact week the class was named.

**Confirmed findings**

- **DEVEX-1** P2 [A/S] — check_main_green.py and check_ci_warnings.py skip every `cancelled` CI/CD run as 'a superseded push with no signal' — but since the 2026-08-02 per-run workflow group nothing supersedes a CI/CD run; a `cancelled` rollup on main is now minted by the deploy job's `ci-cd-deploy-<ref>` concurrency group cancelling the older WAITING Deploy when the next run's Deploy queues (GitHub's one-pending-slot rule), while `test / Unit Tests` in that same run FAILED. Two live specimens on 2026-09-04 carried a real coverage-gate failure that both instruments discard.

Fix the CONVENTIONS §4 sentence in the same PR. · extends: none

Adjacent runs prove the mechanism: 33843452894 Deploy cancelled 06:29:11 == 33843742114 Deploy started 06:29:10; 33843742114 Deploy cancelled 06:33:51 == 33844077590 Deploy started 06:33:51 (group `ci-cd-deploy-${{ github.ref }}`, ci-cd.yml:861-862; workflow group is per-run at :129 since commit 908417617 / #200
  - corrected root cause: Two skips read the run ROLLUP (`conclusion == cancelled`) as 'superseded push'; since #2009 (2026-08-01) the only thing that cancels a main CI/CD run is the deploy job's `ci-cd-deploy-<ref>` group evicting the older pending Deploy when the next run's Deploy queues, and Deploy does not `needs` the Unit Tests job, so the rollup goes `cancelled` while a real test failure is still in flight. `head_coverage()` then classifies the cancelled HEAD run as `pending` (an OK state), so the wrap gate stays g
- **DEVEX-2** P3 [A/S] — docs-ci.yml runs on push-to-main with `concurrency: group: Docs CI-<ref>, cancel-in-progress: true`, so the reconcile bot's follow-up commit (48 s after every test-adding merge) cancels the human commit's Docs CI mid-run: 17 of the last 40 Docs CI runs on main are `cancelled`, and the live specimen at 17143485 (#3483) had `Literal-drift gate (sync_doc_metadata --check)` = FAILURE with every other step green — a real red rendered as `cancelled`. By construction every merge that moves a bot-owned literal gets its own Docs CI verdict destroyed by the commit that fixes it, and any OTHER real red in that run is destroyed with it.

  - reproduction: `gh run list --workflow 'Docs CI' --branch main --limit 40 --json databaseId,conclusion,headSha --jq '.[] | select(.conclusion=="cancelled") | "\(.databaseId) \(.headSha[0:8])"' | wc -l; gh run view 33935174277 --json jobs --jq '.jobs[] | "\(.conclusion) \(.name)", (.steps[] | "  \(.conclusion)\t\(.name)")'`
  - class: a CI-minutes optimization applied to the main-branch verdict stream — cancel-in-progress erases per-commit verdicts on a branch where no run is ever a duplicate · guard: tests/test_docs_ci_owns_doc_gates.py: parse docs-ci.yml and assert the concurrency group includes `${{ github.sha }}` (or cancel-in-progress is `${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}`) so a push to main can never cancel another push's run; keep cancel-in-progress for PR refs only. Same assertion for v4-gate.yml (identical block, :44-46). · extends: none
  - verifier: Evidence reproduces: 16 of the last 40 Docs CI runs on main are `cancelled` (finder said 17 — the window moved by one); run 33935174277 (17143485) job `cancelled Wiki drift gates` with step `failure Literal-drift gate (sync_doc_metadata --check)` and all 11 other gates success, and ALL 16 steps including 'Complete job' finished before the cancel stamp; bot run 33935216861 (9709ac65) success created 48 s later; docs-ci.yml:152-156 and v4-gate.yml:42-46 carry `cancel-in-progress: true` on group `<
  - corrected root cause: cancel-in-progress on a push-to-main group turns a completed-and-failed job into a `cancelled` rollup whenever the reconcile bot (or any push) lands within ~90 s; the loss is attribution and rollup honesty, not tree verification — the superseding run verifies a superset tree. Rent justification for the cancel (#1453 CI minutes) no longer applies on a public repo.

### growth — Growth PM — **C+** (prior C+)

**Anchors (loaded verbatim from fullreview_grades_2026-08-02_delta.json):** A — Every shareable page emits a current, data-driven OG card; the subscribe flow is double-opt-in, canary-tested, AND its conversion (attempts → confirms → list size) is visible in the weekly traffic digest; subscribers get a fixed cadence that matches the promise copy on /subscribe/; engagement hooks (predict, board-ask, votes) work on Day 1 of a fresh cycle and are discoverable from the pages newcomers land on; commercial option value (list, RSS, gear, podcast) is real and measured with zero dishonest monetization.

_Extension:_ EXTENSION (not a redefinition): the F clause 'share cards present wrong/stale numbers as current' is read to cover the whole share surface — /moments/index.json, /moments/share-kits/*/kit.json and the /journal/posts/week-NN/ permalinks those kits canonicalise — because a previous cycle's article served at its public URL with no notice, or a share kit that still advertises a voided cycle's Week 1, is the same defect as a stale card. Also: the C clause 'stale card' is read to include a hard-coded placeholder card on a topic page whose API serves null (og-glucose).

**Trend:** C+ (2026-08-02) → C+: both clauses that earned the prior C+ are genuinely closed (the kill switch is lifted with a live guard, the funnel is in the Monday digest), but the story/share surface today serves prior-cycle content as current — every reset leaves week-03/04/05 articles live because the archive step is slug-idempotent, and the resurrected cycle-15 'Week 1 — 328.1' share kit survived the #3485 repair — which is the F anchor's 'stale numbers as current' on the syndication path, while the subscriber promise ('one email a week, every Wednesday') is still false on cadence, day and count; the machinery is better, the honesty of what a newcomer can be handed is not yet.

- **G-7** P3 [A/S] — SEO/shareability hygiene: sitemap.xml lists a non-page fragment URL (/journal/essays/org-chart-of-one/body — body.html, the essay's verbatim prose fragment, passes indexable() and the URL 301s) and both /subscribe and /subscribe/ (site/subscribe.html is a meta-refresh stub with no noindex or canonical); 66 of 90 v5 pages carry no RSS autodiscovery <link>, and /cockpit/ has no canonical.

Local scan of 90 site/**/index.html (legacy excluded): 'no rss link' 66, 'no canonical' ['site/cockpit/index.html', 'site/subscribe/confirm/index.html'].
  - reproduction: `curl -s https://averagejoematt.com/sitemap.xml | grep -o '<loc>[^<]*' | grep -E 'body|subscribe'; curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' https://averagejoematt.com/journal/essays/org-chart-of-one/body; grep -c 'noindex\|canonical' site/subscribe.html`
  - class: a generator that enumerates files instead of the page registry (tests/qa_manifest.py is THE page registry per the charter) — the sitemap is a second, drifting vocabulary of pages · guard: v4_build_sitemap derives from qa_manifest (the page registry) or excludes non-index fragments and meta-refresh stubs structurally; a test asserts every sitemap <loc> returns 200 and is its own canonical; RSS autodiscovery emitted by the shared head partial. · extends: none

Local scan of 90 site/**/index.html (legacy excluded): no RSS autodiscovery 66, no canonical ['site/cockpit/index.html','site/subscribe/confirm/index.html']; live /cockpit/ head has 0 canonical/rss links. sc
  - corrected root cause: Two defects, not one: (1) the generator enumerates files rather than the page registry (finder's class, correct), AND (2) url_for() strips .html for non-index files, producing extensionless URLs CloudFront resolves as 301→/…/→404 — so the fragment is not merely indexed, it is a dead sitemap entry that also feeds tests/site_review_bindings._sitemap_routes.
- **G-8** P3 [B/S] — The subscriber funnel cannot distinguish a live pending from a dead one: the two real pendings from 2026-07-03 (tokens expired 2026-07-05) are still status=pending_confirmation 63 days later because the retention sweep (RETENTION_WINDOW_DAYS=0) only touches unsubscribed rows and nothing ever expires a pending. The #1954 digest section therefore reports the same 'oldest pending' every Monday forever and a pending count that never decays — the funnel is visible (prior C clause fixed) but its attempts→confirms ratio is contaminated by rows that can never convert.
  - evidence: DDB query pk=USER#matthew#SOURCE#subscribers (projection status/created_at/token_expires): count 3 → 1 confirmed (2026-03-28), 2 pending_confirmation created 2026-07-03T23:45Z / 23:50Z with token_expires 2026-07-05. lambdas/content/subscriber_retention.py:16 'Active (pending/confirmed) subscribers are NEVER touched', :44 RETENTION_WINDOW_DAYS = 0, :61 'Active pending/confirmed rows are never eligible'. CloudWatch /aws/lambda/life-platform-delete-user-data 2026-08-24 and 2026-08-31 08:00Z 'retention_sweep_complete {mode: anonymize …}'. lambdas/operational/traffic_digest_lambda.py:367-375 oldest_pending computed over every pending_confirmation row. lambdas/web/email_subscriber_lambda.py:514-517 expired tokens are only rejected at confirm time, never transitioned.
  - reproduction: `aws dynamodb query --region us-west-2 --table-name life-platform --key-condition-expression 'pk = :pk' --expression-attribute-values '{":pk":{"S":"USER#matthew#SOURCE#subscribers"}}' --projection-expression '#s,created_at,token_expires' --expression-attribute-names '{"#s":"status"}' --output json | python3 -c "import json,sys;[print({k:list(v.values())[0] for k,v in i.items()}) for i in json.load(`
  - class: a lifecycle state with no terminal transition — the token expires but the row does not, so the measurement surface built on it (#1954) cannot decay · guard: The weekly sweep transitions pending rows past token_expires+7d to status=expired (privacy: email hash only, already the case); aggregate_subscriber_funnel counts expired separately and oldest_pending excludes them; a fixture with an expired pending must show 0 live pendings. · extends: none
  - verifier: Reproduced live (read-only DDB query, pk=USER#matthew#SOURCE#subscribers, Count 3): 1 confirmed (2026-03-28), 2 pending_confirmation created 2026-07-03T23:45Z/23:50Z with token_expires 2026-07-05 — 61 days past expiry today. Both pending rows still carry plaintext email (checked as a boolean only: email_present=True, anonymized=False on all 3 rows). Code reproduces: lambdas/content/subscriber_retention.py:44 RETENTION_WINDOW_DAYS = 0, :61 is_retention_eligible requires status == 'unsubscribed' —
  - corrected root cause: A lifecycle state machine with no terminal transition for pending_confirmation: expiry is checked only on the confirm read path, the sweep's eligibility predicate is status=='unsubscribed' only, and the signed DATA_GOVERNANCE row (#3044) classifies every pending row as 'active' regardless of token state. The permanent digest warning is a symptom; the retained unconfirmed plaintext PII is the larger consequence.

  - class: a brand/deliverability decision made by env-var default rather than a registry — the sender identity vocabulary has two homes (code default vs CDK env) and one of them is unsendable · guard: One SENDER constant in lambdas/common (registry), a test that every default and every CDK EMAIL_SENDER value's domain is in a committed list of SES-verified identities, and — owner act — verify averagejoematt.com in SES with Easy DKIM + custom MAIL FROM so reader mail is From the site's own domain. Rent: three DNS records, no standing cost. · extends: none
  - verifier: Every evidence line re-measured read-only (domains masked): aws sesv2 list-email-identities → 2 identities, both DOMAIN type on personal domains; 'averagejoematt.com' in ids → False; get-email-identity averagejoematt.com → NotFoundException. The sending identity: VerifiedForSendingStatus True, DkimAttributes.Status SUCCESS, MailFromAttributes {BehaviorOnMxFailure: USE_DEFAULT_VALUE} (no custom MAIL FROM); dig TXT → 1 SPF record, no amazonses include; _dmarc → 'v=DMARC1; p=quarantine; pct=100; ad
  - corrected root cause: Not a latent hard failure — lambda_helpers.py:176 guarantees the env var, making the between_chronicle default unreachable. The real residue: (a) no ADR records the decision to send reader mail from a personal domain, and (b) one stale code default (from 61e71eadf) names an SES identity that does not exist, a vocabulary defect only.

**Coverage:** Looked at (all read-only, all reproduced live 2026-09-04 ~18:00–19:30 PT): /subscribe/, /subscribe/confirm/, the DDB subscriber partition (hashed rows only), SES account/identity/suppression state and DNS SPF/DMARC/DKIM, the live EXTERNAL_EMAILS_ENABLED and EMAIL_SENDER env on every Lambda, 21–35 days of CloudWatch logs for chronicle-email-sender / weekly-signal / between-chronicle / chronicle-approve / traffic-digest / delete-user-data, the traffic digest's funnel code, all 14 referenced OG cards (headers + 6 rendered PNGs viewed), the local og:image/og:description/canonical/RSS-link scan of 90 v5 pages, rss.xml + every linked item, sitemap.xml + robots.txt, /api/status, /api/journey, /api/predict_week, /api/predictions, /api/experiments, /api/challenges, /api/experiment_library, /api/challenge_catalog, /api/glucose, /api/board_ask (GET), generated/public_stats.json, generated/journal/posts.json (+ its S3 version history), the /journal/posts/week-00..06 permalinks, /moments/index.json, share kits and cards, the reset's chronicle archive step, /gear/ affiliate disclosure, footer social links (3 of 4 profiles confirmed live; Reddit 403 is a bot block), and the closed-issue list + #3485. NOT done: no POSTs (subscribe, votes, follows, board_ask, predict) — the read-only contract — so confirmation-email deliverability/inbox placement, the vote/follow write paths and rate limits were assessed from source and logs only; no rendered email HTML was inspected; no WebSearch market comparison (monetization readiness graded on the site's own disclosure posture, not against competitors); no Core Web Vitals / schema.org / Lighthouse pass; the podcast feed (/story/panel/) and the podcast prequel were not audited; the weekly-plate and nutrition-review red status rows (62d/69d) were noticed but left to the platform row; the 1,011-unique week (≥900 surge threshold, ADR-133) was not traced into the budget governor (cost row); the 01:21Z S3 repair writer is attributed from #3485's own comment (Session V), not CloudTrail (S3 data events are not logged). No pytest was run.
